## 2. Purpose and Scope

Purpose: prove, route by route, that every one of the 369 AMI routes and 4 WebSocket routes can be handed to roadmap Phases 7–24 (`docs/product/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md` L39–L141) with its contract authority, authorization, error, versioning, database, transaction, concurrency, event, provider, dependency and test obligations already fixed by frozen sources — so that no implementation phase has to invent a decision.

In scope: reconciliation of AMI + AAM + AEC + AVS + FAR + owner documents 6A–6M; classification of every route; registers for the 21 IR, 4 RT and 83 FUTURE items; the Phase 7–24 handoff matrix; the Phase 7 entry gate; test obligations; validation evidence.

Out of scope (normative): implementation code of any kind; design of Phase 7 Event Architecture; new routes, codes, permissions, fields, tables or phases; changes to roadmap numbering; resolution of any FUTURE item; any statement that a runtime is implemented. Every runtime referred to in this document is **NOT IMPLEMENTED** unless explicitly stated otherwise (and nothing is so stated).

"READY TO IMPLEMENT" means the contract and all authorities exist. It **never** means built.

### 3.1 Owner-approved AIR closure decisions (normative)

**Default rule.** API Implementation Readiness is subordinate to the frozen Phase-1–6 authorities by default and MUST NOT independently redesign or override them.

**Exception — explicit owner-approved AIR closure decisions:** where §34 records a named owner-approved decision that exists specifically to resolve an identified frozen-source gap, ambiguity, or contradiction, that decision is the authoritative implementation reconciliation for that exact issue and scope.

**Scope limit.** Such a decision supersedes only the conflicting/undefined point explicitly named in its decision record. It MUST NOT be generalized to unrelated behavior, routes, domains, schemas, permissions, errors, or lifecycle rules. No owner decision overrides any source document as a whole.

**Residual rule.** For every matter not expressly covered by one of these owner-approved exceptions, the normal frozen-source authority hierarchy (§3 ranks 1–8) continues unchanged.

**Behavior statement.** AIR introduces no unapproved behavior. It primarily reconciles frozen contracts and adds no independent design. The only implementation behavior it resolves beyond an unambiguous frozen source is behavior explicitly authorized by the owner decisions in §34.

Register of owner-approved exceptions (closed list — exactly seven):

| Decision | Decision record | Exact issue / scope | Authoritative implementation reconciliation |
|---|---|---|---|
| AIR-OD-01 | §34.1.1 | AMI-6C-018 invitation resend invalidation (`DEP-6C-12`) | invitation resend uses the owner-approved membership linkage design |
| AIR-OD-02 | §34.1.2 | `FR-FLAG-001` / `DBGAP-6M-01` roadmap ownership | FR-FLAG-001 remains V1 Release-Train, owned by the cross-cutting V1 Platform Foundation workstream |
| AIR-P0-EVT-01 | §34.1.3 | AMI-6F-007 / AMI-6F-008 `document.uploaded` producer | `document.uploaded` producer = AMI-6F-008 `/complete` only |
| AIR-P0-EVT-02 | §34.1.4 | AMI-6F-012 reprocess signal | reprocess emits no new durable domain event |
| AIR-P0-EVT-03 | §34.1.5 | AMI-6H-007 `campaign.started` producer | `campaign.started` producer = executor PREPARING→RUNNING |
| AIR-P0-EVT-04 | §34.1.6 | AMI-6J-004 non-OAuth (API_KEY / BASIC / CUSTOM) activation and `integration.connected` | non-OAuth activation occurs in the post-commit validation/activation worker; HTTP create remains CONNECTING |
| AIR-OD-03 | §34.1.7 | `action_kind` of the 14 audited routes listed in §34.6 | exact 14-route audit action-kind mapping defined by §34.6 |

OWNER-APPROVED AIR CLOSURE DECISIONS = 7/7 AUTHORITATIVE WITHIN THEIR RECORDED SCOPE

**Immutability.** This authority rule does not authorize modifying 6A–6M, FAR, AMI, AAM, AEC, AVS, the Phase 1–5 documents, `docs/product/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, the 5K SQL migrations or the Alembic revisions. Source amendments: **0** (§34.4). Where a frozen source still carries the superseded wording, that wording remains in the frozen file unchanged; the register above states which reconciliation implementation follows for that exact point.

Conflict examples (the owner decision is authoritative for the named point only):

| # | Point | Frozen wording that implementation MUST NOT follow for this point | Authoritative decision | Implementation follows |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Campaign start — `campaign.started` (AMI-6H-007) | 6H L371 / L374 and 6H L1742 "`POST /campaigns/{id}/start` (on `PREPARING` commit)" — emission on the request's PREPARING commit; 6H L1543 places it on `PREPARING → RUNNING` (F-23) | AIR-P0-EVT-03 (§34.1.5) | The campaign executor emits `campaign.started` on PREPARING → RUNNING, in the same transaction as that transition, with the final `total_contacts`. The Start request commits PREPARING, writes no outbox row and enqueues `prepare_campaign_contacts_task` |
| 2 | Knowledge reprocess (AMI-6F-012) | 6F L523 "`document.uploaded`-shaped re-run" (and the 4E `IngestionJobRetried` concept it names) | AIR-P0-EVT-02 (§34.1.4) | No new durable reprocess event: `document.uploaded` is not re-emitted and `IngestionJobRetried` is not emitted. The request records the reprocess and enqueues ingestion after commit; the ingestion worker emits `document.indexed` / `document.ingestion_failed` |
| 3 | Non-OAuth activation (AMI-6J-004) | 6J L210 "CONNECTING → ACTIVE (credential granted: OAuth callback success, or immediate for API_KEY/BASIC/CUSTOM)" — the "immediate" branch | AIR-P0-EVT-04 (§34.1.6) | HTTP create → `201 CONNECTING` → post-commit provider validation → activation worker → ACTIVE (`integrations.fn_activate_integration_connection`, `101_5I1.sql:280`, exactly one `integration.connected` outbox row) or FAILED (`integrations.fn_fail_integration_connection`, `101_5I1.sql:327`, no event). The request never returns or sets ACTIVE and never emits |
| 4 | Audit vocabulary (14 routes, §34.6) | The centrally maintained 5J audit-vocabulary documentation, which does not yet list the nine new values | AIR-OD-03 (§34.1.7) | The 14-route mapping in §34.6 is owner-approved; the nine new values are authorized for exactly those routes now, even though 5J is not yet synchronized. The governance obligation to synchronize 5J before production through controlled change management remains; this document does not modify 5J, and the values are not authorized for any other route |

## 4. Definitions and Status Vocabulary

| Status | Meaning | Blocks Phase-6 closure? |
|---|---|---|
| READY TO IMPLEMENT | Contract, authz, errors, versioning, DB owner and destination all exist in frozen sources. NOT built. | No |
| READY WITH IMPLEMENTATION OBLIGATION | As above, but the route depends on at least one FAR-IR runtime obligation (§28) that the destination phase must build. NOT built. | No |
| RELEASE-TRAIN — REQUIRED FOR V1 GA | FAR-RT item (§29). Required before V1 GA; does not block development start. | No |
| FUTURE — NON-BLOCKING | FAR-FUTURE item (§30). Not V1. Current routes must tolerate its absence. | No |
| READY WITH OWNER-APPROVED IMPLEMENTATION OBLIGATION | Design resolved by a recorded owner decision (§34.1); the schema/runtime it requires is **NOT YET IMPLEMENTED** and the capability is not complete until schema + logic + tests exist. | No |
| IMPLEMENTATION DEPENDENCY | FAR-IR item: a runtime/provider/config deliverable the source defers to implementation. | No |
| BLOCKED | Implementation cannot be deterministic from frozen sources. Phase-6 closure blocker. | **Yes** — count in this document: **0** |

Markers: **NOT IMPLEMENTED** = no runtime exists in the repository (the repository contains design documents and migrations only). **NOT SPECIFIED IN FROZEN SOURCE** = the cell is carried verbatim from AMI where the source leaves it blank.

Registry legends: `TX-RO` / `TX-1` / `TX-SEQ` / `TX-NET` / `TX-AUDIT` / `TX-EXT` semantic transaction class derived from the endpoint contract, never from the HTTP method (§12); `TP-nn` atomic-unit pattern (§12.4); `CC-nn` concurrency scenario (§13); `T-*` test obligations (§33): T-CONTRACT, T-AUTHZ (authz-negative), T-ERR, T-VER, T-TENANT, T-TXN, T-EXTIO (no transaction open across external I/O; sequential-commit crash safety), T-IDEM, T-CONC, T-AUDIT (only where §18.1 requires audit), T-SIG, T-REPLAY, T-BILL, T-IR.

## 9. WebSocket Readiness

The 4 WebSocket routes are **not** counted in the 369. Transport is raw WebSocket (6A §27.1 L700–704, ADR-6A-05); **no Socket.IO server** is built. WS delivery is realtime-only and **not durable**: the durable source of truth is always the REST resource + outbox (6A §27.2; 6I §41 L882 "non-authoritative push of existing state"). All WS runtimes are **NOT IMPLEMENTED**.

| WS ID | Path | AMI / AAM / AEC / AVS | AuthN / AuthZ | Frames / envelope | Close / error semantics | Limits | Axis | Status | Dependency | Primary phase | Tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

WS versioning: AX-C `/ws/v{major}` (AVS §10 L843, WSP-01..WSP-13). Event `version` field is AX-D and is coupled to AX-C only for breaking changes (AVS-OD-11). WS deprecation signalling (WSP-13) is FUTURE and non-blocking.

## 10. Callback Readiness

All 5 callback routes are counted in the 369 (surface `CALLBACK`). Rule (AAM §11 L648, L125): **callbacks carry no bearer; any tenant bearer header is ignored**; each request stands or falls on signature/state verification; tenant is resolved only after verification and is never caller-selected. Failure semantics are governed by AEC §12 L555, not by the per-route AEC §15 cells (4 of the 5 AEC route rows list no codes: AMI-6D-021, AMI-6J-011, AMI-6J-014, AMI-6K-023). Versioning: AX-G, provider-native, lifecycle per AVS-OD-05 (register successor → accept both → verify both → confirm provider cutover → remove old). Receipt persistence for provider webhooks: `webhooks.inbound_webhook_events` `062_5I:38`.

| CB | Route | Verification | Success / error model | Replay / dedup | AAM | Phase | Tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CB-1 | AMI-6B-013 GET `/api/v1/auth/oauth/{provider}/callback` | OAuth `code` + `state` (6B L1518/L1528); C-STATE | three-shape success (6B L1527); AEC §12 | single-use `state` | AAM #14 L701 | P8 (IR DEP-6B-04 IdP) | T-SIG T-REPLAY T-AUTHZ T-ERR |
| CB-2 | AMI-6D-021 POST `/webhooks/voice/{provider_slug}/events` | provider-native signature per adapter (6D §10.4 L404); C-PROV | status/envelope SOURCE-UNSPECIFIED (AEC-F-15) — implement per AEC §12 only; do not invent | `inbound_webhook_events` receipt + dedup (6A §28.2) | AAM #100 L787 | P9 (+P18 receipt store) | T-SIG T-REPLAY T-IDEM T-CONC(CC-05, CC-14) |
| CB-3 | AMI-6J-011 GET `/api/v1/integrations/oauth/{definition_key}/callback` | single-use `state` bound per 6J §13.2 L445, 10-min TTL L458; callback 6J §13.4 L493; C-STATE | 302 redirect (6J L498); 400 `OAUTH_PROVIDER_MISMATCH` | single-use state | AAM #264 L951 | P18 | T-SIG T-REPLAY T-ERR |
| CB-4 | AMI-6J-014 POST `/api/v1/integrations/providers/{provider_slug}/callbacks/{opaque_connection_route_id}` | opaque route id + connection verification (6J L980–985); C-PROV | 2xx fast-ACK (6J L965/L989), async processing | receipt persistence + dedup (AEC §12) | AAM #267 L954 | P18 | T-SIG T-REPLAY T-IDEM |
| CB-5 | AMI-6K-023 POST `/api/v1/billing/payment-providers/{provider_slug}/webhook` | `verify_webhook` with platform-held secret (6K L2356); C-PROV | fast-ACK async (6K L2499); internal outcomes `PAYMENT_FAILED` / `AMOUNT_MISMATCH` / currency mismatch never trust provider amount over server record | receipt persistence + dedup (CC-14) | AAM #316 L1003 | P20 | T-SIG T-REPLAY T-IDEM T-BILL |

Normative DB rules for implementers: (1) every tenant-scoped table is accessed under RLS with the tenant GUC set by the request pipeline; the application MUST NOT rely on RLS alone where AAM names an explicit permission (sensitive media, §15); (2) SECURITY DEFINER functions are the only write path where they exist (quota admission, refunds, break-glass, quota overrides) — the implementation MUST call them rather than re-implement their SQL; (3) immutability triggers on version tables (010_5C:51, 040_5G:50, 042_5G:53, 036_5F:77, 013_5C:73) MUST NOT be bypassed; (4) no Phase-7+ phase may add a migration `113` without a separate authorised amendment.

## 12. Transaction Boundary Readiness

### 12.1 Invariant (replaces the withdrawn method-derived rule)

Authoritative invariant (6A §35 L910–L935): **no PostgreSQL transaction is held open while waiting on external I/O** (identity provider, payment/telephony/integration provider, secret manager, object storage, email). A request MAY commit **several sequential short transactions** (6A L615 "DB transaction(s)"; canonical example AMI-6J-011) provided none spans an external call. The domain write and any outbox row the owner source requires commit in the same transaction as each other. **No distributed transactions are introduced** (no 2PC, no XA). The rule previously stated here ("one request = at most one PostgreSQL transaction"; "every non-GET route is TX-00") is **withdrawn**: it was derived from the HTTP method and contradicted the frozen contracts (finding P0-AIR-01, §37.1).

Classification method: every one of the 369 routes was classified from its owner contract (Database / Side Effects / Audit / Latency fields, owner transaction section), **not** from its HTTP method. A GET that writes (callback, access-granting read) is not read-only; a POST that only reads is not a mutation. Where the frozen source does not specify an internal ordering, the route is marked "internals left to implementation" and only the 6A §35 invariant is asserted — no internals are manufactured.

### 12.2 Taxonomy

| Class | Definition | Routes |
|---|---|---|
| TX-RO | No persistent write. DB read only (or no DB access at all where the source says `Database: None`). No transaction-scoped side effect. | 175 |
| TX-1 | One short atomic PostgreSQL transaction containing the state change and, where the source requires it, the synchronous audit row and the outbox row. Any post-commit continuation (Celery task, outbox consumer, provider dispatch, reconciler, saga, email) runs outside and after it. | 179 |
| TX-SEQ | Multiple sequential committed transactions inside one request with no external I/O between them. Crash between commits is designed to be retry-safe by the owner source. | 1 |
| TX-NET | Short transaction(s) and external I/O (IdP / provider / secret manager / object storage) in one request: external call is made with no DB transaction open, then a later short transaction records the result. | 11 |
| TX-AUDIT | Read (or guarded read function) plus a durable audit write in the same short transaction; no business-state mutation. | 3 |
| TX-EXT | External-authority-only operation with no PostgreSQL participation. Retained for completeness; the frozen source assigns no route to it. | 0 |
| **Σ** |  | 369 |

| Business-state mutation routes | 191 | = persistent-mutation routes (TX-1 / TX-NET / TX-SEQ, incl. mutating GET callbacks and provider callbacks). **A mutation is not an event producer**: event emission is classified per route in §18.2 from the owner events section, never from mutation status |

### 12.5 Post-commit continuations (outside the request transaction)

These routes are TX-1 (or TX-NET) for the request; the continuation below runs **after** commit and never inside the request transaction. 6D call control (AMI-6D-001, 6D-004…007) is the 6D §23.3 default single-aggregate shape: the response returns once the CAS/INSERT commits and `TelephonyPort` is dispatched afterwards; provider confirmation arrives as a later, independent transaction via the provider webhook (AMI-6D-021).

The capacity reservation of TP-06/CC-05 is an IR runtime obligation (6K §54.5) — its absence is disclosed, not hidden, and no transaction is invented to cover it.

## 13. Concurrency Readiness

The capacity runtime (reservation store, grace period, reconciler cadence) is **NOT IMPLEMENTED** and is an IR obligation (6K §54.5, FAR §11.2). The DB provides the capacity quota resolver and overrides; it does not provide the runtime reservation.

Implementation rules: envelope per AEC §3 L68; precedence 401 → 403 → 404-concealment per AEC §8 L479; retryability per AEC §9 L490; internal categories G (`INTERNAL_NORMALIZATION_CLASS`), H (`INTERNAL_DOMAIN_FAILURE`) and N (`DB_SQLSTATE`) **never appear on the wire** (AEC §11 L540). "409 CONFLICT" prose normalises to `STATE_CONFLICT` (AEC-F-57, AEC L1322). `API_VERSION_SUNSET` (AVS-OD-01) is a FUTURE registration used only at an AX-A sunset; it is **not** a V1 blocker and is not required by any V1 route. No code is invented by this document.

Further rules: axes advance independently (AVS-OD-03); idempotency keys are isolated per major (AVS-OD-09); numeric rate-limit changes with unchanged signalling are operational, not contract changes (AVS-OD-10 carve-out). AVS owner decisions AVS-OD-01..11 are all RESOLVED (AVS §38 L1605). Route axis coverage: AX-A 360, AX-B 4, AX-G 5 = 369; mismatches between AVS surface and AMI surface: 0.

## 18. Event / Async Readiness

Eight mechanisms are frozen as **distinct**. 6A §28.3 L778 ("Three Distinct Mechanisms — Not to Be Confused") separates API response, WebSocket event and outbound webhook; the owner documents add the other five. All eight are **NOT IMPLEMENTED**. Phase 7 (Event Architecture, roadmap L39) implements this table. It does not redesign it.

| # | Category | Durability / delivery | Frozen authority | Producers → consumers (frozen) | P7 may resolve (implementation choice inside the contract) | Frozen — P7 must not change |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Transactional outbox | Durable; written in the producer's DB transaction; at-least-once, never exactly-once (6C L259) | `077_5J1:48` `domain_event_outbox`; `audit.fn_claim_outbox_events()` `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` (6C L257); FAR §7 L300 | Every owner domain's "Domain Events / Outbox" section (e.g. 6F L261, 6G §30, 6H §36, 6I §43, 6J §37, 6K §45) → relay → Redis Streams topics | Relay process topology, poll interval, batch size, claim lease duration, relay metrics | Outbox row committed in the same transaction as the state change; consumers idempotent; no exactly-once claim; event names and payloads as owner docs define them |
| 2 | Redis Streams | Transport, **not** a source of truth; certain classes are never outbox-routed (6D L1234) | Relay → topic (6C L235); Redis role frozen by 6A §21/§28 | Outbox relay and the 6D voice direct-stream classes → P9, P12, P13, P18, P19, P20 consumers | Stream naming, consumer-group layout, retention (MAXLEN), pending-entry reclaim | Redis is not authoritative for durable state; a WS-only or direct-stream event is never also claimed as durable |
| 3 | Celery async jobs | Durable job row; `202` + job resource contract (6A §18.1 L473) | 6A §18 L471; enqueue "if and only if this statement actually returns a row" (6J L953); SLAs indicative only (6A R-6, IR) | 6A-001 job polling; 6F ingestion/reindex; 6G CSV/merge; 6H import/materialization; 6J delivery/sync; 6K invoicing | Queue names, worker pools, prefetch, visibility timeouts, autoscaling | No Celery task ID exposed as an API identifier; job state read from the DB job row; retries respect the owner idempotency contract |
| 4 | APScheduler (in-process scheduling) | Timer trigger only; the durable effect goes through the owner's transaction | Campaign Executor "(Celery/APScheduler, in-process application-service calls — never a REST hop)" (6H L221) | 6H calling-window/retry scheduling (6H §11, §20) → 6H application services | Scheduler placement, misfire grace, leader election | A scheduler never calls the platform's own REST API; timers never become the source of truth |
| 5 | Public (outbound) webhooks | Signed, at-least-once, dead-letter (6A L778) | 6J §17 L611–6J §23 L888; `062_5I:3`, `063_5I:5`; AVS AX-E (envelope) / AX-F (`v1=` signature) | Outbox → 6J delivery worker → tenant endpoints; 6J-provided replay APIs | Worker concurrency, backoff jitter within the 6J schedule, egress pool | Signature scheme `v1=` (AVS-OD-08, no in-place mutation), envelope, retry/backoff contract, dead-letter semantics, the external event catalog (6J §19) |

| 7 | Audit events | **Mixed, per 5J §14.5 (L808–L835)**: synchronous in the business transaction (failure rolls the mutation back) for auth, API keys, break-glass, DSR, admin, the 15 ‡ Voice/Agent/Tool operations and the ❖ guarded-function billing operations; **asynchronous** (Celery, post-commit, retried; never best-effort) for configuration, campaign, plugin, ordinary billing and ordinary CRM operations. The hash chain is **not** computed at write time: the nightly job `audit.fn_compute_chain_hash()` computes it separately into `audit.audit_chain` (5J §15 L843; nightly job 5J L154 / L892) | `072_5J:17` `audit_events`, `072_5J:71` chain; 5J §14.5 L808–L835; per-owner audit sections (§18.1) | Only the routes that §18.1 lists as **AUDIT_SYNC / AUDIT_ASYNC / AUDIT_CONDITIONAL** (source-derived per owner audit section and 5J §14.5, never per HTTP method) → `audit.audit_events`; read by 6L-012/013/014, 6M audit routes | Nothing on the write path. P7 does not own audit. | Audit is **not** a domain event and is not delivered by the outbox (6I §43 L926 durable vs telemetry); the per-route sync/async mode of §18.1; an async audit write is retried, never best-effort; the chain is computed by the nightly job, never inline |

### 18.1 Audit obligations (source-derived, P0-AIR-04)

Each of the 369 routes is placed in exactly one audit class from its owner audit section, the route-table Audit field and **5J §14.5 (L808–L835)**, **not** from its HTTP method and never by a global "audit = synchronous" rule. 5J §14.5 is applied exactly: auth, API keys, break-glass, DSR and admin → synchronous (failure rolls back); configuration / campaign / plugin lifecycle → asynchronous (Celery) unless marked ‡ (15 Voice/Agent/Tool operations); billing → asynchronous except the ❖ guarded-function operations. Narrower owner rules are preserved (6I §42.3 synchronous exception; 6G erasure/suppression synchronous; 6J §36 per-action). A read that grants sensitive access is audited (AMI-6D-011, 6M-008, 6M-009); a POST that only reads or validates is not (AMI-6B-033, 6C-004, 6I-006). The 14 routes whose owner source names no exact `action_kind` carry the owner-approved AIR-OD-03 hybrid mapping (§34.1.7, §34.6); no other value is introduced.

### 18.2 Event / outbox classification (source-derived, P0-AIR-05)

A mutation is **not** an event producer. Each route is classified from its owner "Domain Events / Outbox" section; no event name is invented, and a route whose owner catalog names no event is OUTBOX_NONE. Only OUTBOX_REQUIRED and OUTBOX_CONDITIONAL routes can themselves create an `audit.domain_event_outbox` row in the request transaction (`077_5J1:48`); these, and only these, carry `CC-15` (§13). Worker-emitted events are attributed to the worker, never to the HTTP request that enqueued it. An audit section is never used as an event catalog.

| Event class | Count | Meaning | Routes (AMI-) |
|---|---|---|---|
| OUTBOX_REQUIRED | 75 | the request transaction writes the outbox row for a catalogued event | 6B-009, 6C-001, 6C-030, 6D-001, 6D-004, 6D-006, 6D-007, 6D-012, 6E-001, 6E-004, 6E-005, 6E-006, 6E-007, 6E-011, 6E-013, 6E-014, 6F-001, 6F-004, 6F-005, 6F-006, 6F-008, 6F-009, 6F-014, 6G-001, 6G-004, 6G-005, 6G-006, 6G-007, 6G-008, 6G-011, 6G-013, 6G-023, 6G-024, 6G-027, 6G-029, 6G-033, 6G-034, 6G-035, 6G-036, 6G-044, 6G-048, 6G-052, 6G-053, 6G-055, 6G-061, 6G-062, 6G-065, 6G-066, 6G-067, 6G-068, 6G-069, 6G-075, 6G-078, 6H-001, 6H-004, 6H-005, 6H-006, 6H-008, 6H-009, 6H-010, 6H-011, 6H-017, 6I-001, 6I-005, 6I-007, 6I-008, 6J-007, 6J-018, 6J-032, 6J-036, 6J-037, 6J-040, 6K-006, 6K-007, 6K-008 |
| OUTBOX_CONDITIONAL | 2 | outbox row only on a named branch | 6B-036, 6J-011 |
| OUTBOX_WORKER_EMITTED | 1 | the request enqueues/advances work; a worker transaction emits the event | 6H-018 |
| OUTBOX_NONE | 265 | no event (reads; owner catalog names none; audit-only) | 265 routes — §8 column "Event/outbox/audit" |
| DIRECT_REDIS_STREAM | 20 | internal-bus event, not outbox-persisted (6C §20 L1046–L1061) | 6C-003, 6C-006, 6C-007, 6C-010, 6C-011, 6C-012, 6C-013, 6C-014, 6C-015, 6C-016, 6C-021, 6C-023, 6C-024, 6C-026, 6C-027, 6C-031, 6C-034, 6C-035, 6C-036, 6C-037 |
| WS_ONLY | 1 | non-durable WebSocket push only | 6D-005 |
| PUBLIC_WEBHOOK_DELIVERY | 2 | outbound tenant-webhook delivery (test / replay) — not a domain event | 6J-025, 6J-028 |
| PROVIDER_CALLBACK | 3 | inbound provider callback; processing after fast-ACK | 6D-021, 6J-014, 6K-023 |
| EVENT_TRIGGER_UNRESOLVED | 0 | none — AIR-P0-EVT-01…04 resolved by owner decision (§34.5) | — |
| **Σ** | 369 | must equal 369 | — |

Routes that can create an outbox row = OUTBOX_REQUIRED 75 + OUTBOX_CONDITIONAL 2 = **77** (the `CC-15` set). Business-state mutation routes 191; audit-only durable-write routes 3; any persistent write 194. The 191 mutations are **not** the producer set.

### 19.1 SIP trunk support — RELEASE-TRAIN (V1), `RR-SIP`

Source: 6L L1123, "**E. SIP trunk support (V1 requirement)** — additive to the existing `TelephonyPort` abstraction and Exotel implementation, **not a replacement** … **Not designed, not scoped, not implemented in this 6L pass**". FAR §11.1 L977 lists it as RELEASE-TRAIN. Mechanical check: none of the 369 AMI paths or the 4 WS paths contains `sip`. The API surface is **missing**, so every requirement below is registered as `RR-SIP` (release-readiness gap). **No route, table or field is invented here.** SIP stays V1 RELEASE-TRAIN and is never demoted to FUTURE.

Column meaning: **Contract complete** = the API/behavior contract is frozen in an owner document. **DB complete** = the table/function/constraint exists in migrations 001–112. **Runtime required** = the implementation phase must still build it; in every case the runtime is NOT IMPLEMENTED.

**Money is server-authoritative.** Four frozen rules guarantee it: payment amounts are computed server-side at payment-intent creation (6K §29.4 L2188); the provider is chosen by platform policy, not the client (6K §29.2 L2180); webhook tenant resolution never trusts the payload first (6K §30.3 L2390); and the settled amount/currency is compared against the server-computed attempt (6K §30.6 L2407, `PAYMENT_AMOUNT_MISMATCH` is webhook-only and internal, 6K L2548). Any implementation that accepts a client-supplied price, amount, currency, rate or minute count as authoritative violates the contract. Validation reason: `PRICING_CLIENT`.

**Capacity is not implemented.** The configuration functions exist in `112_5H5`. The admission gauge (acquire/release/reconcile, fail-closed `UNAVAILABLE`) does not exist anywhere in the repository. 6K §54.5 L3676 fixes its semantics and leaves the storage/locking mechanism open to the implementation phase. Validation reason: `CAPACITY_IMPLEMENTED`.

## 27. Analytics, Audit and Admin Readiness

Owners: 6L (analytics, audit), 6M (platform admin). Primary phases: P19 Analytics Platform (roadmap L111) and P8 Authentication & Authorization for 6M authorization. A **query contract is not population.** The 6L endpoint contracts are frozen, but 10 of the 12 projections have no executed population function (6L L815; 6L §12 L161). That gap is IR `6L §55-5` (§28). All projection workers are **NOT IMPLEMENTED**.

Common to every analytics endpoint: freshness is reported, not guaranteed. Responses carry `data_as_of` / `is_partial` (6L §16 L217; 6L L226); the data is **eventually consistent**. Tenant visibility is under RLS, with `analytics:read` for tenant routes (6L §11 L130). `analytics_platform:read` is granted to **zero** tenant system roles (6L L153). Provider cost/margin is internal-only (6L §28 L443).

**Sensitive media** (recordings, transcripts) is purpose-controlled. 6M §21 L185: recordings 6M §21.1 L187, transcripts 6M §21.2 L191, column-restricted base grants 6M §21.3 L197, endpoints 6M §21.4 L201, restricted column list 6M §21.6 L215. Platform-admin access goes only through break-glass `C-BG-SM` via `107_5B5:931` / `107_5B5:987`, after `105_5B4:200` against a grant from `107_5B5:174`. The tenant media routes are RLS-scoped, and **content** access needs the purpose permission on top of read: AMI-6D-011 `recording:access_media + recording:read` (AAM PA: DENY 403); AMI-6D-014 `transcript:access_content + transcript:read` (AAM PA: DENY 403). AMI-6D-013 returns transcript metadata only (`transcript:read`).  No analytics projection holds media content. Validation reason: `SENSITIVE_MEDIA_AUTHZ`.

### 27.1 Historical Rebuild / Backfill (IR `6M §52`)

Source: 6M §52 L607, row 6M L616: "Requires a backfill-control primitive not itself specified by this document; deferred to implementation". 6L states that no rebuild/replay/backfill control API exists for tenants or generic admins (6L L37), and that historical rebuild uses isolated staging (6L L666). **The primitive is UNSPECIFIED. This document does not invent one.**

| Aspect | Frozen position | Status |
|---|---|---|
| Owner | 6M (§52 handoff) for any platform-admin trigger; 6L owns projection semantics | Owner known |
| Affected projections | All 12 `analytics.*` projections (`call_metrics_hourly`, `call_latency_stage_hourly`, `conversation_turn_stats_daily`, `usage_cost_daily`, `agent_utilization_hourly`, `lead_funnel_daily`, `campaign_outcome_summary`, `tool_execution_stats_daily`, `webhook_delivery_stats_daily`, `roi_by_campaign`, `billing_revenue_monthly`, `provider_health_5min`) | Known |
| Authorization boundary | No tenant or generic-admin API (6L L37). A future platform-admin trigger belongs to 6M (6L L666). | Frozen: PA-only if it ever exists; no V1 route |
| Audit | Any PA-triggered action is audited per the 6M audit contract (§18 row 7) | Frozen by general rule |
| Idempotency / retry | Projection application is claim-and-mutate via `analytics.fn_claim_projection_slot` (`068_5J:113`); 90-day replay horizon (6L §47 L674) | Per-event idempotency exists; the rebuild orchestration is UNSPECIFIED |
| Isolation | Rebuild into isolated staging per 5J §12.3 (6L L666) | Frozen principle; mechanism UNSPECIFIED |
| Production safety | Rebuild never mutates live projections in place (isolated staging); no tenant-triggerable path | Frozen principle |
| Relation to P19 / P21 / P24 | P19 builds projections and producers; P21 observes projection lag/freshness; P24 is the production-readiness gate for any operational rebuild procedure | Derived mapping (§31) |
| Primitive | **UNSPECIFIED** — no table, function, route or job is defined | IR; destination P19. Not a blocker: no V1 API depends on it. |

Status: 20 of 21 IR items are **READY WITH IMPLEMENTATION OBLIGATION**; `DEP-6C-12` is **READY WITH OWNER-APPROVED IMPLEMENTATION OBLIGATION** (AIR-OD-01, §34.1.1). Readiness here means the design is deterministic from frozen sources plus recorded owner decisions; it does not mean any migration is written, any runtime exists, any test has run, any provider is selected or any RT item is complete.

Totals: 21 IR items. 13 are DEP rows (6B×2, 6C×1, 6D×3, 6F×4, 6K×3) and 8 are non-DEP (6A R-4, R-6; 6I §54-5, §54-10; 6K §54.5, §54.6; 6L §55-5; 6M §52). 0 prevent P7. 0 require an owner decision now. 2 carry a known **future** owner decision (DEP-6D-05, DEP-6D-10; §34.2) that is not a blocker. DEP-6C-12 carries the owner-approved implementation obligation AIR-OD-01 (§34.1.1).

### 31.1 Dependency DAG (phase level)

```
P7 Event ──► P9 P10 P11 P12 P13 P17 P18 P19 P20   (event-driven domains)
P8 Auth  ──► every phase that exposes a route (almost all)
P9 Voice ──► P12 Campaign, P19 call analytics
P10 RAG  ──► P9/E agent knowledge (6E knowledge_base_refs)
P14 Prompt, P15 Memory, P16 LLM, P17 Tool ──► agent runtime in P9
P11 CRM  ──► P12 campaign audience
P13 Workflow ──► P18 integrations / tool nodes (and P18 ──► 6I §54-5 egress)
P20 Billing ──► quota + capacity for P9, P10, P12, P16
P19 Analytics ◄── producers P9, P12, P13, P17, P18, P20
P21 Observability, P23 Testing ── cross-cutting
P24 Production ◄── all
```

The graph is acyclic at phase level. The only mutual reference, P13 ↔ P18, is broken by direction: 6J egress (P18) is the prerequisite, and workflow node publish (P13) is the consumer.

### 31.2 Vertical-Slice Build Order (per domain)

A route is to be given only the layers its frozen source requires. A pure read (TX-RO) gets no transaction, audit or outbox machinery. A mutating GET or callback (AMI-6B-013, 6J-011) and an audited read (AMI-6D-011) are never built as read-only.

| Step | Layer | Applies to | Exit evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | DB / infra | all routes | migrations 001–112 frozen; an approved implementation DB obligation (AIR-OD-01 in the P8 invitation slice; AIR-OD-02 in its workstream) is materialized only in its owning slice, never here; repository connects under the right role and RLS |
| 2 | Repository | routes with a DB authority (§11.1) | repository + DB-integration + RLS tests |
| 3 | Domain / service | all routes | unit tests; §12 transaction class honoured; TX-NET/TX-SEQ never hold a transaction across external I/O |
| 4 | Authz / security | all routes | authn, authz-negative, tenant-isolation tests (AAM row) |
| 5 | API / event adapter | all routes | API-contract test against the owner doc; AVS axis |
| 6 | Errors | all routes | error-contract test against the AEC row |
| 7 | Audit where required | §18.1 AUDIT_SYNC (94), AUDIT_ASYNC (84) and AUDIT_CONDITIONAL (6) routes only | AUDIT_SYNC: audit row in the **same** transaction as the state change or access grant (failure rolls back); AUDIT_ASYNC: audit written **post-commit by a Celery task** (retried), never inside the request transaction; AUDIT_CONDITIONAL: only on the named branch; AUDIT_NONE: none. The hash chain is never computed inline (nightly job, 5J §15) |
| 8 | Outbox only for real producers | §18.2 OUTBOX_REQUIRED + OUTBOX_CONDITIONAL (77) routes only | outbox row in the same transaction as the domain write (conditional: only on the named branch); **none** for OUTBOX_NONE, worker-emitted (the worker writes it), direct-stream, WS-only, webhook-delivery, callback and read routes; the former AIR-P0-EVT-01…04 routes follow the owner-selected producers of §34.5 |
| 9 | Tests | all routes | the §33 matrix rows for the slice |
| 10 | Freeze gate | all routes | slice reviewed against this document before the next slice |

Build one end-to-end slice per domain. Do **not** generate 369 empty route shells: an unimplemented shell has no DB, authz or error behavior, so it would pass nothing in §33 and would hide readiness gaps.

## 32. Phase 7 (Event Architecture) Entry Gate

Question (spec §13): **Can Phase 7 — Event Architecture begin after Phase 6 closes?** The gate is fourteen proofs, each computed from the §18.1 audit and §18.2 event classifications, the §13 register or a frozen anchor; none is derived from the HTTP method or from "state-changing". The gate is recomputed, never assumed. This section does **not** design Phase 7. Phase 7 is roadmap P7 Event Architecture (`PROJECT_ROADMAP.md` L39).

| # | Proof | Evidence (frozen anchor / computed) | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The transactional outbox contract exists | `077_5J1:48` `audit.domain_event_outbox`; claim function `audit.fn_claim_outbox_events()` (6C L257); delivery at-least-once, never exactly-once (6C L259); FAR §7 L300. DB baseline: 112 SQL / 112 Alembic revisions, root 001_5B, head 112_5H5, linear, `113` absent | PASS |
| 2 | Exact producers are named | Routes that can create an outbox row = OUTBOX_REQUIRED 75 + OUTBOX_CONDITIONAL 2 = **77**, each with a named event, producer, transition, consumer and owner evidence (§18.2). This is **not** the 191 business-mutation routes and **not** the 194 non-GET routes | PASS |
| 3 | No-event routes are explicit | OUTBOX_NONE 265 routes each carry a named reason (§18.2); pure / read-derived non-GETs (AMI-6B-033, AMI-6C-004, AMI-6I-006) and audit-only routes (AMI-6D-011, AMI-6M-008, AMI-6M-009) are not producers — an audit row is not a domain event | PASS |
| 4 | Worker-emitted events are not attributed to the request | OUTBOX_WORKER_EMITTED 1 (AMI-6H-018): the worker writes the outbox row, the request writes none; 10 request routes carry a worker note (§18.2) and no worker-only event name appears in a request producer row | PASS |
| 5 | Provider I/O boundaries are identified | 12 TX-NET/TX-SEQ routes (§12.3/§12.4) make external calls with **no DB transaction open**; 29 routes have a post-commit continuation outside the request transaction (§12.5); 6 routes leave internals to implementation under the 6A §35 invariant (§12.6) | PASS |
| 6 | Redis Streams and WS are distinct from the outbox | DIRECT_REDIS_STREAM 20 (6C internal bus, non-durable) and WS_ONLY 1 + 4 WS channels are not outbox producers; WS push is not durable (6A L778); Redis Streams is transport, not truth (6D L1234) | PASS |
| 7 | Audit is distinct from events | Audit is a **mixed** contract (5J §14.5 L808-L835): AUDIT_SYNC 94, AUDIT_ASYNC 84, AUDIT_CONDITIONAL 6, AUDIT_DOMAIN/PROVIDER_PROCESSING 3; the audited set ≠ the producer set; audit is **not** a domain event (6I §43 L926); tamper evidence is the nightly hash chain `audit.fn_compute_chain_hash()` (5J §15) | PASS |
| 8 | Public webhooks and provider callbacks are distinct | PUBLIC_WEBHOOK_DELIVERY 2 (outbound delivery, AX-E/AX-F) and PROVIDER_CALLBACK 3 (inbound, fast-ACK, AX-G) are not request producers; all 5 callbacks classified: AMI-6B-013 OUTBOX_NONE; AMI-6D-021 PROVIDER_CALLBACK; AMI-6J-011 OUTBOX_CONDITIONAL; AMI-6J-014 PROVIDER_CALLBACK; AMI-6K-023 PROVIDER_CALLBACK | PASS |
| 9 | No fabricated event names | every `x.y` token of every producer event is found verbatim in its owner document; fabricated = 0 | PASS |
| 10 | No N/A route is a producer | AMI-6F-017 (§31 L1025 "N/A") and AMI-6F-021 (no §31 row) are OUTBOX_NONE; AMI-6B-005 (refresh / reuse) is not a producer | PASS |
| 11 | The outbox concurrency scenario covers exactly the producers | §13 `CC-15` outbox publication in a 15-row register CC-01…CC-15; CC-15 routes = 77 = producers 77 | PASS |
| 12 | Phase 7 need not invent frozen API behavior outside the register | All 21 IR items: "Prevents P7?" = No (§28, machine-asserted); Phase 7 adds no route, error code, permission, table or migration | PASS |
| 13 | Every unresolved trigger is registered | EVENT_TRIGGER_UNRESOLVED routes: none; the former triggers AIR-P0-EVT-01…04 (AMI-6F-007, AMI-6F-008, AMI-6F-012, AMI-6H-007, AMI-6J-004) are resolved by owner decision (§34.5); none hidden | PASS |
| 14 | No event trigger is unresolved | EVENT_TRIGGER_UNRESOLVED = **0** — AIR-P0-EVT-01…04 resolved by owner decision (§34.5) | PASS |

**PHASE 7 ENTRY GATE = PASS** — 14/14 proofs pass

What Phase 7 inherits (frozen; it may not change these): the outbox table and claim function; at-least-once delivery; the distinct mechanisms of §18; the per-owner event catalogs; the §18.2 per-route event classification (77 producers, the rest named no-event, worker, stream, WS, webhook or callback); AX-D/AX-E/AX-F versioning of event, webhook and signature; and the rule that WS is non-durable. What Phase 7 decides (implementation choices inside the contract, §18 column 6): stream naming, consumer groups, retention, relay polling cadence and worker topology. Phase 7 must not add routes, error codes, permissions, tables or migrations, and must honour the owner-selected producers of §34.5. This document starts no Phase 7 work.

## 34. Blocker, Owner-Decision and Amendment Register

A blocker is a gap where implementation cannot be made deterministic from frozen sources (spec §33). Each criterion below is computed from the registry or anchored to a frozen resolution. A FUTURE item is never counted as a blocker, an RT item is never demoted to FUTURE, and a known **future** owner decision is never counted as a current blocker.

| Blocker criterion (spec §33) | Count | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Undefined permission (empty AAM permission cell) | 0 | §15 computed check |
| Error code absent from AEC registry | 0 | §16: route codes not in AEC §5 registry |
| Nonexistent DB authority (empty DB owner cell) | 0 | §8 column 16; §11; §11.1 (unresolved blocker class = 0) |
| Security invariant conflict (8 authz checks) | 0 | §15 computed checks, all expected 0 |
| V1 provider behavior without an implementable contract | 0 | 117 provider-touching routes all cite a contract; SIP is RT (§29), inbound decline signalling is IR `6K §54.6` (§28) |
| Contradictory money / quota authority | 0 | single admission authority, same effective limit (6K L3500); FAR-OD-03/04/05 resolved (FAR L17) |
| Impossible transaction boundary | 0 | §12: 369/369 routes semantically classified; 0 transactions span external I/O (TX-NET/TX-SEQ, §12.2) |
| Missing protocol choice | 0 | 6A L702 (raw WebSocket); ADR-6A-09 (6A L631); AX-F `v1=` (§17) |
| Event trigger not derivable from frozen sources (EVENT_TRIGGER_UNRESOLVED) | 0 | none — AIR-P0-EVT-01…04 resolved by owner decision (§34.1.3–§34.1.6, §34.5) |
| Unresolved owner decision (including AIR-P0-EVT-01…04, now resolved) | 0 | FAR L17; AAM L1493; AVS-OD-01..11 resolved (AVS §38); AIR-OD-01 and AIR-OD-02 resolved by the owner (§34.1); AIR-OD-03 resolved by the owner — owner-approved hybrid mapping (§34.1.7, §34.6) |

**Blockers: 0.**

#### 34.1.1 AIR-OD-01 — invitation resend invalidation (owner decision = Option A)

| Field | Record |
|---|---|
| Decision | **AIR-OD-01 = OPTION A** (owner-approved, binding; not reopened by this document). Extend the existing invitation-purpose token persistence with a nullable `membership_id` linkage, reusing the existing token subsystem (`002_5B:77`); add the referential, index and uniqueness constraints needed so that after an invitation resend exactly one valid invitation token for that invitation remains valid |
| Classification | **OWNER-APPROVED IMPLEMENTATION OBLIGATION** (`DEP-6C-12`, IR, 6C L1177) |
| Affected route | AMI-6C-018 `POST /api/v1/organizations/{organization_id}/invitations/{membership_id}/resend` — §8 status `READY WITH OWNER-APPROVED IMPLEMENTATION OBLIGATION`; §11.1 DB authority class "implementation DB obligation approved but not materialized" |
| Design readiness | **Resolved** — the design question is closed by the owner; no owner decision is required |
| Runtime / DB completion | **NOT YET IMPLEMENTED** — schema, model, logic and tests do not exist. The resend route is not implemented (and the guarantee MUST NOT be claimed) until migration + logic + tests exist |
| Implementation obligations | (1) additive migration on the existing invitation-purpose token persistence (nullable `membership_id` + referential, index and uniqueness constraints); (2) model update; (3) membership-scoped invalidation query; (4) issue the new token and invalidate prior tokens together in one short transaction; (5) concurrency safety for concurrent resends; (6) tests (below) |
| Migration | **NOT YET IMPLEMENTED.** No migration is created by this remediation; `113` MUST NOT be created now and is absent (§36 A). The implementing phase (P8) authors it under normal change control |
| Completion gate | Complete only when the schema, the invalidation logic and all tests below exist and pass |
| Concurrency invariant (CC-02) | After any sequence of sequential or concurrent resends, **exactly one current valid invitation token** for that membership remains; concurrent resends **MUST NOT leave multiple valid tokens**; no cross-membership invalidation |
| Required tests | sequential resend; concurrent resend race; prior token rejected after resend; membership linkage; the same user invited to multiple organizations; no cross-membership invalidation; expiration; used token |
| API impact | None — no route, error code, version, permission or AAM cell is added or changed; cancel/accept remain safe (6C §9.4 L475) |

| Affected route(s) | AMI-6F-007 `POST /api/v1/knowledge-bases/{kb_id}/documents/upload-url`, AMI-6F-008 `POST /api/v1/knowledge-bases/{kb_id}/documents/{document_id}/complete` |
| Former source gap | 4E §11.1 L990 / L1174 vs. 6F L337 (PENDING insert) / L353 (PENDING → PROCESSING) / L473 / L1021 — the sources name the event once but assign it to neither route |
| Owner decision | **Option B** — AMI-6F-008 emits `document.uploaded` exactly once on PENDING → PROCESSING (§34.1.3) |
| Producer | AMI-6F-008 request transaction: same-transaction `audit.domain_event_outbox` INSERT; ingestion enqueued after commit; no S3 HEAD, hash, parse or embedding while the transaction is open |
| Resulting classification | AMI-6F-007 → OUTBOX_NONE + AUDIT_NONE; AMI-6F-008 → OUTBOX_REQUIRED + AUDIT_ASYNC `DOCUMENT_UPLOADED` (sole writer) |
| Consumers | Analytics 6L, Billing 6K (dedupe on `event_id`) |
| Destination | P7 (outbox) + P10 knowledge owner phase |
| Status | **RESOLVED — owner decision = B** |

#### AIR-P0-EVT-02 — re-ingestion signal for reprocess

| Field | Record |
|---|---|
| Affected route(s) | AMI-6F-012 `POST /api/v1/knowledge-bases/{kb_id}/documents/{document_id}/reprocess` |
| Former source gap | 6F L523 (Knowledge-specific equivalent inferred, not catalogued); 6F L1013 (no reprocess row) |
| Owner decision | **Option C** — no request event (§34.1.4) |
| Producer | none from the request: reprocess directly enqueues ingestion; the ingestion worker emits `document.indexed` / `document.ingestion_failed`. `document.uploaded` is not re-emitted; `IngestionJobRetried` is a 4E DDD concept only |
| Resulting classification | AMI-6F-012 → OUTBOX_NONE with a worker note; audit unchanged (AUDIT_ASYNC `DOCUMENT_REPROCESS_REQUESTED`) |
| Destination | P7 + P10 |
| Status | **RESOLVED — owner decision = C** |

#### AIR-P0-EVT-03 — `campaign.started`

| Field | Record |
|---|---|
| Affected route(s) | AMI-6H-007 `POST /api/v1/campaigns/{campaign_id}/start` |
| Former source gap | 6H L1742 / L371 / L374 (request, PREPARING commit) vs. 6H L1543 (executor, PREPARING → RUNNING, with `total_contacts`) vs. 6H L1595 (no outbox write for Start) |
| Owner decision | **Option B** — the campaign executor emits on PREPARING → RUNNING (§34.1.5) |
| Producer | campaign executor, in the PREPARING → RUNNING transaction, with the final `total_contacts`. The request validates, pins the AgentVersion, sets PREPARING, commits and enqueues `prepare_campaign_contacts_task`; it writes no outbox row |
| Resulting classification | AMI-6H-007 → OUTBOX_NONE with a worker note; audit unchanged (AUDIT_ASYNC `CAMPAIGN_STARTED`) |
| Consumers | Billing 6K ("start cost tracking") |
| Destination | P7 + P12 campaign owner phase |
| Status | **RESOLVED — owner decision = B** |

#### AIR-P0-EVT-04 — `integration.connected` (API_KEY / BASIC / CUSTOM)

| Field | Record |
|---|---|
| Affected route(s) | AMI-6J-004 `POST /api/v1/integrations/connections` |
| Former source gap | 6J L210 (immediate ACTIVE) vs. 6J L311 (201 CONNECTING) vs. 6J L384 (no activation step); only the OAuth flow names the emission step (6J L441) |
| Owner decision | **Option B** — a post-commit validation/activation job activates and emits (§34.1.6) |
| Producer | activation worker: validates outside any DB transaction; on success one short transaction calls `integrations.fn_activate_integration_connection` (`101_5I1.sql:280`) and writes one `integration.connected` outbox row; on failure `integrations.fn_fail_integration_connection` (`101_5I1.sql:327`) sets FAILED, no event |
| Resulting classification | AMI-6J-004 → OUTBOX_NONE with a worker note; returns `201 CONNECTING`, never ACTIVE; audit unchanged (AUDIT_SYNC `INTEGRATION_CONNECTION_CREATED`) |
| Consumers | WebhookDispatchService (6J §37); internal only, not webhook-eligible (6J L1916) |
| Destination | P7 + P18 integrations owner phase |
| Status | **RESOLVED — owner decision = B** |

### 34.6 AIR-OD-03 — audit action kinds (RESOLVED — OWNER-APPROVED HYBRID MAPPING)

These 14 routes are audited (class fixed by 5J §14.5 and the owner section, §18.1), and the owner source named no exact `action_kind`. The owner has approved the hybrid mapping below (§34.1.7): five routes reuse an existing action kind and nine use a new owner-approved action kind. No SQL migration is needed: `action_kind` is `TEXT NOT NULL` with a length-only constraint (`072_5J.sql` L23, L39).
